  - ⭐ **THE SWI ROW, DONE BY COMPUTED RECEIPT at SCRIP 14cbc23c1 (the held pair squashed onto origin):**
    - Cause: `freeStream()` in pl-file.c (406-411) sets `source_file_name = NULL` and `source_line_no = -1` whenever a closed stream's filename equals the file being loaded. '$mon_line' opening and closing the program file inside '$store_clause' therefore left `record_clause` asserting on line -1.
    - Cure: '$mon_line' saves `source_location/2` and restores it with `'$set_source_location'/2` in the cleanup after close.
    - Found by MONDBG prints in the build tree's init.pl (a 2 s boot rebuild). A first probe with `'$set_source_location'('/x', 7)` survived open/get_char/close, because the reset needs the SAME file name.
    - Measured on the build script's own artefact (fork 117 s at load 26-32): the three witnesses AGREE at 5/24/27; sync_step_prolog 9/57/16 AGREE; _4 DIVERGES at 35 on swx AND gpx; control arm equal to pristine on four witnesses; two monitor gates PASS; 2-way all-langs 5/5; preflight 60 arms 0 red (70 s at load 34); allocator ratchet arm (d) 0 worse.

    - Design: CALL in the VM at pl-vmi.c `depart_or_retry_continue` for predicates flagged `P_MON_CALL` (FLAG64(40)). STMT and RETURN are injected by `boot/init.pl` `'$store_clause'`, with lines from the reader's subterm positions. The VM alone cannot do STMT: it keeps no per-goal line, and `=`, `!`, `==` and `\+` compile inline with no port. The loader injects only when the READY pipe is named.

  - ⭐ **THE 11 ARE ALL hq_prolog's `6a6983d9e`** (`by_name_dispatch.c:3045-3094`, `wot_open/wot_close/wot_discard`, the `with_output_to` capture): the census's own `--sites` list and a per-line `git blame` agree. ⛔ **The ceo's record splits them across three seats, and two of those hold zero at HEAD `fb57f00ba`.** hq_pascal's `9c0a5760c` was named because `git log -S'free('` matches `fh_free(`, since the pickaxe is a substring search. hq_raku's `d9c66f2c6` was FULLY reverted by `c1f3431d0` (all five lines), not partly. hq_pascal was right, and I told them so.
  - ⛔⭐⭐ **THE HAZARD:** five `open_memstream` sites take a libc-owned buffer. Four of them release it with `ct_drop`: format/3 at `by_name_dispatch.c:3328`, `unification.c:981`, `unification.c:1729/1731`, and `core.c:489`. The eradication sweep `c6a2c07e1` (CEO-842) rewrote those from `free` (the diff reads `-free(bp)` / `+ct_drop(bp)`). `ct_drop` (`ct_arena.c:89-107`) reads a header 32 bytes below `p` and returns without freeing when there is no CT magic. **MEASURED** with a failure-driven `format(atom(A),…)` loop, ASLR off: **63.5 MB → 208.6 MB → 1,662 MB at 2k / 20k / 200k calls, a linear ~8.1 KB per call**, against a flat 47 MB `atom_concat` control. The same slope holds at `SCRIP_HEAP_MB=1`. **SIGSEGV at 200k with ASLR on: 3 of 6 runs at the default arena and 3 of 4 at 1 MB. With ASLR off: 0 of 4.** gdb with randomization on caught `ct_drop(p=0x79368ad00010)` at `ct_arena.c:94`, called from `plc_fb_term` `unification.c:981`. `p` is base+16 of a fresh 1 MB glibc mmap segment and `p-32` falls in the unmapped gap below it. **The leak and the crash are one defect.**
  - ⛔ **THE INVERSION:** hq_prolog's libc `free` of its own libc buffer is memory-correct, and the four green siblings are the ones that leak. **Copying format/3's `ct_drop(buf)` into `wot_close` greens the blocking gate and adds a fifth crash-capable site with every census quiet.** Direction only, not a ruling: take no libc-owned block at all (for example a runtime-owned `fopencookie` stream). The form and the row belong to the ceo and the cto.
  - ⛔ **THREE INSTRUMENT BLIND SPOTS (the coo's area):** (1) `util_c_allocator_census.py:258` `_LIBC_OUT_PARAM` has no `open_memstream`, so `LIBC-OWNED-MISUSE=0` reads over four live sites, while RULES.md line 29 (CEO-850) forbids "any future one"; (2) a bare `strdup(nm)` at `by_name_dispatch.c:3178` (`6a6983d9e`) appears in no count; (3) the gate's arm (g) prose "the MAGIC guard makes that safe rather than correct" is measured false, because the guard's own read is the fault.
  - ⭐ **READER CHECKS IN MY AREA (CEO-1202), BOTH CLEAN, NO MAIL NEEDED.** (a) **The cto's collector landings since CFO-152:** one, `ce7669e59` (07:47). It withdraws the `bb_binop_concat_slot:84` poll, which reddened `procedure_every_alt_replace_16` at stress 1 in both modes, and names the site beside the deferred-pattern entry, taking the census baseline unpolled from 1 to 2. That is the sanctioned split-out-and-name move, not a forced cure. Their count reproduces on HEAD `fb57f00ba`: `allocating_call_sites=230 polled=228 unpolled=2`, identity holds, 7.7 s. `util_gc_acceptance.py` VERDICT REFUSE(2) is the design state: INSTRUMENTED 228/230; VISIBILITY 16/16 with one OWED event (`per_visitor_kind_raw_and_gap`, declared at `afc23d068` 09-22, before CFO-151, so not a regression); WORKING unmeasured, with no board pass since 09-20. Under stress all 43 collect (121, 98 and 79 collections in total at 1, 3 and 5), so **the stress points are the only Rebus reading that witnesses the collector.** [[arena-mb-states-a-configuration-not-an-exercise]]

- **CFO-152 (2026-09-23 06:3x -> 07:3x CDT, `date`-read, cfo; MODE DECTET; officers stood down (CEO-1178) except for THIS ROW, which Lon gave me in-chat at 06:3x, verbatim: *"Build the Rakudo IPC sync-step monitor inside Rakudo just like CEO did for Icon and Prolog and get a few hello world type programs working, then hand off to HQ-RAKU to complete so he can use the instrumented Rakudo to find and fix bugs in SCRIP."*; 9 inbound read, 2 answered, cleared) -- THE RAKUDO ORACLE IS INSTRUMENTED IN ITS OWN SOURCE (participant `rkx`), FOUR HELLO-WORLD WITNESSES AGREE WITH SCRIP EVENT-FOR-EVENT, FOUR SCRIP PLUG CURES THE LOCK-STEP EXPOSED ARE LANDED UNDER THE TRACE GATE, AND THE ROW IS HANDED TO hq_raku.**
  - ⭐⭐ **WHAT LANDED (SCRIP `3fd46dd77`, rebased over the coo's fpx landing `220cd725a` and the cto's `0df95bc5f` -- the harness conflict was the two participants' allowed lists, merged to carry both): `scripts/monitor/oracles/{monitor_rkx.c, rakudo-mon.patch, build_rakudo_mon.sh}`, participant `rkx` in the harness, `.raku --oracle` = `rkx scr`, three new witnesses, the rejected stderr-replay bridge (`raku_oracle_bridge.py`, the `.oracle.raku` twin, participant `rko`) DELETED on CEO-1189's word.** The design page carries the mechanics (MONITOR-BINARY-DESIGN.md § THE ORACLES ARE INSTRUMENTED IN THEIR OWN SOURCE, the Rakudo paragraph): four MoarVM extops registered beside Rakudo's own in `perl6_ops.c`, `Ops.nqp` mappings, `Actions.nqp` injection into THE USER'S UNIT ONLY (never CORE, never a precompilation, `RAKUDO_MON=0` off), the fork configured `--with-nqp` against the shared oracle's own MoarVM/NQP so only the Rakudo compiler rebuilds.
  - ⛔⭐ **THE ONE SHAPE THAT BROKE, AND WHY IT IS THE REUSABLE LESSON: a statement hook injected as a SIBLING node in front of the statement broke `make install` of the fork itself** (`Distribution::Hash.new({ name => … })` compiled as a Block: Rakudo's `circumfix:sym<{ }>` decides hash-or-block by reading the block body's `[1][0][0]`, and the CATCH/QUIT handler PUSHES `$_` into `[1][0]`). The hook now lives INSIDE the statement's own `QAST::Stmt` and the one index reader that must see through it is patched. Rule: **an instrumentation node must not change what an index-based reader of the tree finds at its position -- put it inside the node the reader expects, not beside it.** Two builds were spent learning it (the first at a sibling; the second with a one-level-short reader fix).
  - ⭐⭐ **READINGS (RAKUDO_MON_ROOT at my private prefix, `/home/claude_cfo/.scratch/rakudo-mon`; the from-scratch proof prefix built by the script agrees identically):** `sync_step_raku.raku` AGREE at step 21 (was 15 events under the m3/m4 arm before the cures, 21 now); `_2` (say, interpolation, explicit + implicit return, `+=`, if/else, while) AGREE 32; `_3` (recursion, `~=`, nested if in for) AGREE 86; `_4` (array/hash stores, list for, method calls) AGREE 32; two wider probes (tA 36, tB 33 -- `use v6`, bare `my $u;`, arrays, hashes, element stores, implicit return, Rat, Num, Bool, while). `test_monitor_2way_sync_step_all_langs.sh` languages=5 pass=5 (raku 21 steps). `make preflight` 60 arms, 1 red = the standing design-state allocator census (`test_gate_c_allocators_are_eradicated_and_say_where_they_went.sh`), same as the ceo's last two landings. parser-sync gate GREEN after `regenerate_parser_and_lexer_from_sources.sh` (⛔ a hand `bison`/`flex` run is NOT the sanctioned regeneration: the script post-processes bison's allocator prologue into the arena, and the gate reads that as 20 differing lines).
  - ⭐⭐ **THE FOUR SCRIP PLUG CURES THE ORACLE EXPOSED, all under the trace gate, UNTRACED EMISSION PROVEN UNCHANGED:** (a) a statement's line was the line where the parser REDUCED it -- a multi-line `for`/`if`/`while` reported its closing brace (or the next statement's line when the reduce needed a lookahead), and a block's trailing statement without `;` had NO line and so NO event -- `raku.y` now runs `%locations`, the lexer stamps every token's first line (a string literal keeps its opening line), `stmt_list` and every `'{' stmt_list …` trailing form stamp the statement with its FIRST token's line (64 grammar sites through one `exprlist_append_at`); (b) `my $u;` fired VALUE `u = ''` -- the parser marks a bare declaration's null and the plug is silent; (c) `for @a -> $x` fired VALUE `x = …` (the synthesized `__decl` binding), a parameter binding not a store, silent now (a range loop never fired one -- the two loop shapes disagreed with each other before they disagreed with the oracle); (d) `use v6;` fired a STMT -- silent now. And on the wire a `@`/`%`-named store is ARRAY/TABLE with no bytes (SCRIP keeps a Raku array as a `\x01`-joined string, which the wire read as STRING(5)); the text trace keeps its spelling, so hq_raku's byte-identical trace stands. **PROOF:** 889 Raku sources (the master's 883 distinct entries + six probes) compiled to `.s` by a control worktree at the parent and by this tree: **0 differ, 0 rc differ** (19 s). Raku master on this tree, both modes, shipped default arena, S4E_ONE_RUNNER_OVERRIDE named: **m3 868/929 FAIL 15 · m4 866/929 FAIL 8 · 46 xfail_wrong in both, 145.7 s at load 12-14** -- identical to the published RakM row 866/929 (SCORE.md, `19b2517d0`). No score write.
  - ⛔ **NAMED, NOT HIDDEN -- what the oracle side does not cover today (hq_raku's row as each matters):** every value that is not Str/Int/Num/Bool/Array/Hash/Code/Handle goes on the wire as UNKNOWN with no bytes, and ⛔ the controller WILDCARDS TYPE AND VALUE BYTES on UNKNOWN (`keys_match`), so a Rat that SCRIP gets wrong is NOT caught (tB's `my $r = 1/4` agreed by absorption; a Rat wants REAL from its numerator/denominator); `.=`, `||=`/`&&=`/`//=`, attribute stores, `return` with 0 or 2+ values (no event on either side), nameless routines. Also found by the probes and left to hq_raku as SCRIP defects the monitor did not need to grade: `say "$k => %h{$k}"` prints `%h{one}` literally (hash-element interpolation), and SCRIP models `1/4` as a REAL 0.25 where Raku has a Rat.

- **CFO-151 (2026-09-23 01:0x -> 01:4x CDT, `date`-read, cfo; MODE DECTET; 5 inbound read, 4 answered, cleared) -- STEP 0 DONE AND ITS ANSWER RETIRED THE WORK IT WAS SUPPOSED TO CHOOSE THE FORM FOR: THE FRAME MAP READS DESCR, THE CEO'S BARE POLL IS ALREADY EMITTED AT THREE OF MY FOUR SITES, AND THE CENSUS CANNOT SEE IT.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE DECODE, AND IT ANSWERS THE FORM QUESTION FOR EVERY SEAT, NOT JUST CHUNK B.** `.Lgcmap_p$2F1`'s own `.quad` words against `emit_gc_map_data` (`src/emitter/emit.cpp:2960`) and `src/ir/gc_frame_map.h:10-17`: magic `0x50414D5A` ✓, `frame_bytes=192`, `header_bytes=80`, `flags=8` (`GC_FRAME_MAP_LAYOUT`), `map_off=96`, `lay_n=3` = **`[0..80) GC_LAY_DESCR`**, `[80..88) PTR_CODE`, `[88..96) RAW`. `q/1` word-identical; `$fc/3` is `frame_bytes=400 map_off=304 lay_n=8` opening `[0..176) DESCR`. **Offset 64 is inside the DESCR run in ALL THREE graphs holding the four sites**, the anchor is `(map cell) - map_off` so layout offset 64 IS `[rbp+64]`, and `gc_walk_interior` (`src/runtime/rt/gc_heap.c:1167`) dispatches on `GC_LAY_KIND` **alone**, walking a DESCR run at every 16 bytes into `rt_gc_visit_descr`. ⛔ **THE ROLE CANNOT SURVIVE BECAUSE THE ENCODING HAS NO FIELD FOR IT: `src/ir/frame_layout.c:855` MERGES adjacent same-kind runs** -- which is exactly why `p/1`'s five DESCR slots are ONE entry -- and the "SLOT-ELIDE shared dead-result scratch" text exists only in `--dump-zeta`. So: **at a site whose result is spilled to a mapped DESCR slot before the poll, the BARE `x86_rt_gc_poll()` is sufficient and `poll_rec_res` is a second mechanism over covered ground** (CEO-1170 ruled this conditional on the decode; the decode reads DESCR, so no design violation and no row). One live constraint, `gc_tag_known`: `DESCR_t` is `{uint32 v; uint32 slen; void *p}`, so the tag rides rax to +64 and the pointer rdx to +72 and the cell is well-formed only after the SECOND store.
  - ⛔⛔⭐ **AND THEN THE FORM WAS ALREADY THERE. `bb_call_fn.cpp:207` EMITS THE BARE POLL SIX INSTRUCTIONS AFTER THE CALL.** `call rt_pl_dop_unify_cs@PLT` | 3 rtccb reloads | `mov [rbp+64], rax` | `mov [rbp+72], rdx` | `cmp al, 104; je p$2F1_step` | 3 rtccb writebacks | `call rt_gc_poll_asm@PLT`. `:210` identical in `q/1`; `:216` identical, reached with `SCRIP_NO_CU=1`. The poll is the **COMMON TAIL** of a braced `if/else if/else` chain at `:242`, 26-35 source lines down; the census stops its window at the first intervening ARM's call, and its two sibling exemptions read TERNARY arms (`_ternary_sibling_offsets`) and single-line `else if` (`_stmt_sibling_offsets`), never a braced block ONE CHAIN OUT from the site. ⛔ **THE SENSITIVITY KNOB IS INERT: `--poll-window` 12, 20, 30, 40, 60 and 120 ALL read `polled=207 partially_polled=0 unpolled=46`** -- the STOP is the whole story, so anyone who widens the window to check learns nothing.

- **CFO-150 (2026-09-23 00:0x -> 00:5x CDT, `date`-read, cfo; MODE DECTET; 12 inbound read, 12 answered, cleared) -- A LANDING, A CROSS-LANGUAGE EMITTER DEFECT TRIAGED TO THE LINE AND ROUTED, AND TWO CORRECTIONS OF MY OWN THAT BOTH CAME FROM SKIPPING AN INSTRUMENT I HAD JUST WARNED OTHER SEATS ABOUT.**
  - ⭐⭐ **WHAT LANDED, SCRIP `dff0f6e26`: THE CENSUS RECONCILES ALL THREE CLASS LISTS AGAINST THE TALLY, AND A PLANT THAT REDS WITHOUT IT.** My NEXT step 1 was the `CELL:...:UNCLASSIFIED` double-filing. ⛔ **THE FILING RULE ITSELF IS THE cto's, LANDED CONVERGENTLY IN `9806d9e42` WHILE MINE WAS IN FLIGHT** -- same function, same three-way dispatch on `bucket()`; I dropped my duplicate half in the rebase conflict and kept the comment carrying the measured history. Read off `concord.icn.s` graph `n16_scan_enter_α`: `call rt_scan_enter@PLT`, subject in rax and length in rdx spilled to `[rsp+8]`/`[rsp+24]`, poll, reload, `mov r13, rax` / `mov r15, rdx` -- the ScanSubjRegs pair of section 6.5 seeded by Icon's entry. ⛔ **NOT A DEMONSTRATED COLLECTOR BUG** -- at every site I read the value arrives by a reload out of the poll's OWN spill record, so it WAS handed to the collector; it is an INSTRUMENT defect, and exactly the shape this census exists to prevent. Two live consequences: **arm 2b reds on `heap=0`** and is satisfied by 6 SNOBOL4 readings while 270 of the same semantic class sit in UNCLASSIFIED and the copy ratchet; and the census's own source says the spill-record gate reads the heap list as its COVERAGE population, so **that gate reports coverage over a population missing 270 sites**. ⭐ It STRENGTHENS the cto's chosen cure (compile-time IR fact, `rec_sigma` only inside a scanning expression) by 21x rather than weakening it. ⛔⛔ **AND TWO CORRECTIONS TO MY OWN CORRECTION, BOTH MEASURED BEFORE THE CTO COULD BUILD ON IT.** (a) **IT IS 232 DISTINCT SITES, NOT 283** -- 283 is the count of site-by-seed ATTRIBUTIONS and the per-seed sets OVERLAP; the union is 232 = 13 SNOBOL4 + 219 Icon. That is the SAME error class as 4954-vs-4974 which I had already caught once in this very entry, made again in my own correction. (b) ⛔⛔ **WIDENING `SUBJECT_SEED` ALONE MOVES ZERO READINGS**, measured with the census's own window: `classify_def` resolves `mov r13, rax` by looking back EXACTLY TWO INSTRUCTIONS (`range(i-1, max(-1,i-3), -1)`). In-window: 13 def instances, all `rt_match_enter`, which are the 13 ALREADY classed SUBJECT. Out-of-window: **270 def instances over 219 distinct sites** (`rt_scan_enter` 202, `rt_scan_reenter` 66, `rt_scan_reenter_live` 2) -- because between `call rt_scan_enter` and `mov r13, rax` sit the spill of rax/rdx into the poll's record, `call rt_gc_point_arr_c`, four `rtccb` reloads, `add rsp` and a `test`. SNOBOL4's entry happens to sit within two instructions; Icon's never does. **So the cure needs BOTH halves and the second is load-bearing:** the SET {rt_match_enter, rt_scan_enter, rt_scan_reenter, rt_scan_reenter_live}, AND tracing the rax half THROUGH the poll's spill record (the same see-through the r13 cell tracer already does) or widening the window past the rtccb block; `mov r15, rdx` for LENGTH has the identical shape and needs the identical pair. Sent to the cto before their landing, because their stated plan was the widening alone and their re-run would have read SUBJECT unchanged at 13 and scored my number wrong. **Every other number in this entry is unaffected** -- ownership, 4886, 92.8%, the 30-site residual are independent of the SUBJECT/UNCLASSIFIED split, because both classes are non-raw and both were already counted.   - ⛔⛔⭐⭐ **TWO HOLDERS OF ONE ADDRESS, BOTH SITTING BESIDE A REGISTRY THE COLLECTOR ALREADY VISITS.** `kw_read` kept the `&cset` and `&ascii` block ADDRESSES in function-local `static const char *cs` caches -- those two are the only keyword csets whose content carries a NUL byte, so they cannot go through `make_kw_cset`, which reads the registry BY NAME and is therefore always current. `kw_cset_gc_roots` visits `g_kw_cset_names[i].ptr`, so the registry moved correctly at every collection and the statics never did. **MEASURED AT THE FAULT UNDER `setarch -R`: the &cset static holds 0x7fff6ca9d150, bit-identical to the pointer in the faulting DESCR, while the registry entry holds the relocated 0x7fff6ca0c7c0** -- and the &ascii static was stale in the SAME run without faulting, which is the silent half. The second holder is `g_kw_cset_hidx`, the POINTER-KEYED index: the visit rewrites the `.ptr` fields through a registered slot AFTER the root phase and nothing rebuilds the index, so `kw_cset_find_ptr` MISSED a perfectly current pointer, fell through to the content index -- which refuses a string whose first byte is NUL -- and `kw_cset_len` returned -1, i.e. `strlen()` == 0 for both csets. **On origin `every !&cset` yielded 0 to 24 of its 256 elements, rc=0, no diagnostic, in every Icon program that bangs a keyword cset after a collection.**

The 257-byte cset block had been moved off that ground by an EARLIER collection and `repl` took it. The birth site was exact at every step and was never the diagnosis; that sentence was already in the baton from CFO-140 and I still walked into the second instance of it.
  - ⭐ **THE CURE, one file, no allocator touched:** `kw_read` caches the registry SLOT (an int cannot go stale) and reuses the entry `kw_cset_prime` already registered rather than appending a second one; `kw_cset_find_ptr` rebuilds the pointer index when `rt_gc_runs_count()` moves -- outside the collection, one pass per collection rather than per lookup. ⛔ **A FIRST SHAPE OF THE CURE TRIPPED THE LAW'S OWN DETECTOR AND I RESHAPED RATHER THAN BASELINING IT AWAY**: routing both keywords through one helper deleted two `rt_wsb_alloc` sites in a file that carries arena debt, and `test_gate_c_allocators_are_eradicated_and_say_where_they_went` read CLASS-1-EVASION=1 -- *the cure for an unrooted holder is a root, never a different allocator*. It was a false positive (my patch added no arena allocation), but `c_allocator_sites_baseline.tsv` is FROZEN ON PURPOSE -- it records what each site WAS -- so a seat rewriting it to silence its own red is the evasion the rule exists for. The landed shape keeps both allocation sites and caches an index: CLASS-1-EVASION=0, CLASS-2-DEBT back at its baseline 112, no baseline edited.

  - ⛔⭐⭐ **MY OWN INSTRUMENT CAUSED A FLEET-WIDE MISREADING AND I FOUND IT BY BEING HANDED ITS OUTPUT.** `gc_vac_t` stores the allocation site and its caller as RAW RETURN ADDRESSES and the report resolved them with `dladdr`, which returns only `dli_sname` -- **THE ENCLOSING SYMBOL**. `try_call_builtin_by_name_bl_s` is **2125 lines and holds 26 distinct `rt_wsb_alloc` call sites**, so *"allocated by rt_wsb_alloc from try_call_builtin_by_name_bl_s"* is **about as discriminating as naming `main`**. The signatures were never identical; the SYMBOL was.** ⛔ **AND THE `lc_vec` HYPOTHESIS IS DISCONFIRMED** -- neither site is a static `lc_vec`; the four open holders gain nothing from this, which is the cheap early no the ceo asked for.

So the class is **"a WSB result string handed back from an Icon builtin through `*out` is not rooted across a LATER collection"**: ~48 candidate members, two deterministic end-to-end reproducers. ⛔ **I HAVE THE BIRTH SITE EXACTLY AND THE HOLDER NOT AT ALL, and I am recording that rather than rounding an exact site up to a diagnosis.**

  - ⛔ **AND THE MECHANISM I PROPOSED FOR THE LOSS IS MEASURED AND WRONG, RECORDED RATHER THAN QUIETLY REPLACED.** I expected forced relocation to leave less vacated ground for the trap to protect. `[ZGC-POISON]` totals: **213472 bytes off against 214448 on -- within half a percent.** The quarantine is the same size either way. So I hold **no confirmed mechanism**, only a right-shaped hypothesis: **the collector COMPACTS**, so forcing every block to move does not leave the old address empty -- the heap packs from the bottom, a neighbour takes that ground, and the stale read gets a valid title and a plausible payload instead of a fault. **Two entries at three points is an observation and not a rate**, and I did not report it as one. The fresh-region variant -- the GOAL's own other option -- is the successor, written into the baton's NEXT with its acceptance: **not "every block moved", which is already true and already insufficient, but a MEASURED RISE IN LOCATED DEATHS with no fall anywhere.**
  - ⭐ **THE coo's COUNTER ASK ARRIVED AFTER I HAD ALREADY SHIPPED IT, AND THE TWO ROADS CORROBORATE.** Their event enumeration found exactly one collector event nothing could distinguish -- **slide WITH displacement versus slide WITHOUT** -- because `[ZGC-WALK]` prints `slide=li` (all live blocks) and `moved=` in aggregate BYTES. `w_unm` now rides that line, counted where no memmove happens. ⛔ **It is computed in a DIFFERENT PLACE FROM MY CENSUS -- theirs in the SLIDE from which memmove arm declined the block, mine in the FORWARDING loop from `livef[i]` against `liveo[i]` -- and they agree EXACTLY: 896273 off, 0 on.** Neither knows the other exists. Second corroborated pair with the coo in one day.

- **CFO-117 (2026-09-20 14:0x -> 14:3x CDT, `date`-read, cfo; MODE SEPTET, effort max; CFO-116 CURED) -- THE CRASH IS A 16-ENTRY CACHE OF RAW HEAP POINTERS THAT NEVER JOINED AN INVALIDATION PROTOCOL THE COLLECTOR ALREADY CALLS. Seven lines; origin vs cured over a 10-point band, 3 of 3.**
  - **THE CENSUS ENDED THE HUNT IN TWO HOPS.** The chain walk from `_var_buckets[192]` with each hop's BLOCK HEADER printed: hop 0 is a real NV_t (`size=80 type=215`=`HB_WSB`, `name="thy"`), hop 1 is `size=32 type=2` -- and **`DT_S = 0x02`**, so `thy->next` points at a **STRING**. `sizeof(NV_t)=56` read over a 16-byte payload overruns it: `offsetof(cell)=24` lands on the FOLLOWING block's header word, and the garbage `cell=0x1000200000020` decodes exactly as `size=32 type=2 flags=1`, which the linear heap walk confirms. **The garbage cell was never a pointer; it was a neighbour's header.** Printing the block header first is the lesson I had already paid for once and it worked again.
  - ⭐⭐ **A HARDWARE WATCHPOINT NAMED THE WRITER.** Watch on `thy + offsetof(next)=48`; nine writes; the last before the crash is `value=0x7ffe6ce55c90` from **`rt_dcap_pump` at `pattern_match.c:806`, which is `*cell = d`** -- two writes after `gc_collect_ex` compacted over the same address (`memmove`, `gc_heap.c:1150`). The collector slid the heap; `rt_dcap_pump` then wrote 16 bytes through a `DESCR_t *` cached before the slide, onto a live NV_t's `next` field.
  - ⭐⭐⭐ **THE DEFECT IS NOT A MISSING MECHANISM -- IT IS A CACHE THAT OPTED OUT OF ONE ALREADY RUNNING.** The collector's own banner states the law (`[GC-SHIFT] ... a stale copy of any heap address is wrong after every collection`), **`gc_heap.c:1156` already calls `rt_nv_memo_invalidate()`** six lines after the slide, and the sibling cache `_var_find_cached` already joins it via `g_nv_memo_seen[i] == g_nv_memo_gen`. `g_dcap_nv_cell`/`g_dcap_nv_key` never did: their COMPLETE census is 8 references, all in `pattern_match.c`, **not one a root visit, a forwarding update or an invalidation.**
  - ⛔ **THE OBVIOUS ABLATION IS CONTAMINATED AND I RECORD IT SO NOBODY REPEATS IT.** `g_call_fastpath_off=1` (gdb, no rebuild) does remove the crash -- **and also moves stress 0 off the ref**, `3b192bd3` -> `ad07daca`. It gates other fastpaths and is not semantics-preserving, so it cannot attribute anything. A separable two-bit probe did: bit 1 (dcap) **cures the crash**, bit 2 (`g_sno_defer_cells`) **changes nothing**, and the control arm is byte-identical to origin at both levels. The crash is the dcap cache and nothing else.
  - **THE CURE, AND ITS BAND (my own law: a two-point stress DONE-WHEN is a lottery).** `g_dcap_nv_seen[]` compared against `g_nv_memo_gen` at **BOTH** read sites -- the second is the inline fastpath in `rt_dcap_pump` and is exactly the sibling-spelling hazard CFO-111 published a census without. `g_nv_memo_gen` goes `static` -> `visibility("hidden")`, matching `NV_CELL_IF_FASTSET_fn` beside it; **`nm -D` shows it absent from the dynamic table**, so the CFO-114 copy-relocation hazard does not apply. ORIGIN vs CURED, `SCRIP_HEAP_MB=1`, 3 of 3 at ten points: **byte-identical at stress 0/1/2/3/5/8/13/55** (including stress 0 on the ref's own md5 `3b192bd3`), and **rc=139 -> rc=0 at 21 and 35**. It changes nothing it was not aimed at.
  - ⛔ **WHAT IT DOES NOT DO, SAID PLAINLY: it converts a CRASH into a WRONG ANSWER; the entry still does not pass.** `a84e1945` across most of the band is present IDENTICALLY ON ORIGIN, so it is untouched by this cure and is a second ingredient -- **hq_snobol4's row**. CFO-116 suspected the two were separate; this ablation is the proof rather than the suspicion, and the two distinct wrong fingerprints say the wrong answer is itself ragged.
  - ⭐ **THE CLASS IS WIDER THAN THE SITE AND IS NOT MINE TO RULE, and naming is not measuring:** (2) `g_sno_defer_cells[4096]` stores the same pointers **as `uint64_t`**, invisible to any pointer-shaped scan BY CONSTRUCTION -- left uncured on purpose, its `2048 + site*2` layout is `_Static_assert`ed against `rtx_match.s` and a layout change does not belong inside a crash fix; (3) `p->pcells[k]` / `p->rcell` (`rt.c:1258`, `1261`) park `NV_PTR_fn` results in a procedure record that outlives collections; (4) `rtx_match.s:433` reaches the same getter from assembly. **(2), (3) and (4) are NAMED, NOT MEASURED.** Routed to the ceo for ranking.
  - **ARM THAT FAILS IF THE WORK STOPS:** `test_gate_gc_nv_cell_caches_honour_the_memo_generation.sh` -- arm 1 runs the witness through the harness's OWN extract (16 `-INCLUDE` companions asserted present) at the two stress levels that crashed and refuses a signal death; arm 2 requires every `g_dcap_nv_key[]` comparison to carry a `g_dcap_nv_seen[]` beside it, so a future edit cannot generation-check one read site and miss its sibling. **Arm 1 grades rc ONLY, deliberately** -- grading stdout would red on hq_snobol4's open defect and make the arm unreadable.
  - FINDING-2026-09-20-cfo-the-pattern-capture-nv-cell-cache-never-joined-the-collectors-own-invalidation-protocol.md

()` frames share that range, and called it a wild pointer into emitted memory. **`g_hp_arena` PRINTS `0x7ffe6ce00000`** -- the entry and the bucket head are both INSIDE the collected arena and the arena's base moves per run, so comparing an address against a base remembered from a different run is worth nothing. The question to settle first is whether a NULL-name entry can be legitimately linked at all, and then which predecessor's `->next` points at it -- a chain walk from `_var_buckets[192]` with the block titles printed at each hop, which is the census shape that has worked twice on this collector.

- **CFO-115 (2026-09-20 14:2x -> 14:4x CDT, `date`-read, cfo; MODE SEPTET, effort max; CEO-996 point four) -- THE SIXTH `rt_gc_point` SITE IS LOAD-BEARING AND I DID NOT LAND ITS DELETION: `rt_scan_enter` HANDS THE SCAN SUBJECT BACK IN TWO REGISTERS NO MAP COVERS. Tree ends byte-identical to `2facb6c60`.**
  - ⛔ **A CORRECTION TO MY OWN CFO-111 CENSUS FIRST:** it published *"THE COMPLETE COLLECTION-POINT CENSUS, all of `src/`"* and **this site was not in it** -- the census enumerated `rt_gc_point_arr`, and `builtins/gen_runtime.c:69` calls **`rt_gc_point`**, the singular wrapper, the only call of that spelling under `src/`. A census keyed on one spelling of a name missed its sibling. It was complete for the spelling it grepped and not for the thing it named.
  - **PROBE BEFORE BELIEF:** gdb with pending breakpoints, hits counted without stopping over a 3-iteration integer-scan witness -- `descr_to_str_fracdigit` 3, `rt_scan_enter` 3 (`lo=3, hi=987654321`, a DT_I), `rt_gc_point` 3. The witness reaches the allocating call AND the poll.
  - **A/B/A, ONE LINE, ONE FILE, `SCRIP_HEAP_MB=1`:** poll present 217876 / 217876 / 217876 / 217876 at stress 0, 1, 3, 7 (small witness 29) -> **poll deleted 217863 / 37904 / 132636 / 174312 (small witness 9)** -> reverted 217876 at all four, 29. ⛔ **STRESS 0 CHANGES TOO**, so a natural collection at the mandatory arena loses data and this is not a stress artifact.
  - ⭐⭐ **THE MECHANISM IS ARCH-GC 3b FROM THE REGISTER SIDE, ON A SECOND INDEPENDENT ROAD.** The allocator never collects (CFO-113), so no collection can run between the allocation and `scan_subj = s`; deleting the poll moves that collection to AFTER `rt_scan_enter` has returned. At that instant `scan_subj` IS a root and is updated -- **the returned `ScanSubjRegs { ptr, len }` register pair is not**, and the emitted scan machinery reads the pre-collection pointer. hq_snobol4's witness parks a live DESCR in a spine scratch cell below the map; this one hands it back in a register pair no frame map covers at all. **Same clause, different storage class.**
  - ⛔ **AND MY OWN NEW `[GC-SPINE-LOST]` PRINTS ZERO LINES ON THE BROKEN BUILD** while the witness answers 9 instead of 29. That is correct and it is the limit already written into arm 3 on CEO-881's words -- it cannot see a pointer that is only in a REGISTER or only in a C FRAME at the moment of collection. Nobody should read a silent `[GC-SPINE-LOST]` as evidence of health.
  - **WHAT IS OWED, AND IT IS NOT A POLL IN C:** either the scan subject stops travelling as a raw register pair and rides a slot the caller's frame map covers, or the emitted caller re-reads the rooted `scan_subj` after any safe point. Both are the emitter's/planner's region. Routed to the ceo for ranking; **nothing landed**.
  - FINDING-2026-09-20-cfo-the-sixth-safe-point-is-load-bearing-the-scan-subject-goes-home-in-two-registers-no-map-covers.md

  - ⭐⭐ **LANDING 1 (`9410338f8`), `[GC-SPINE-LOST]`:** every raw heap word the SPINE class reports is remembered and re-read AFTER the mark drain and BEFORE forwarding; a block that ends the mark phase UNMARKED is NAMED (graph, offset, address, block, type, first 24 payload bytes). **On hq_snobol4's witness it is exact: fires at stress 1, 2, 4, 8 and silent at 0, 3, 5, 6 -- 4 of 4 reds, 0 of 4 greens**, naming `blk` and `text=EXPR$0$lvl1`.   - ⛔ **AND I CENSUSED IT BEFORE ENJOYING IT, WHICH IS WHY NO GATE REDS ON IT: over the 32 witnesses of `scripts/gc_witnesses` at stress 1 and 1 MB it prints 1954 LINES OVER TWELVE WITNESSES THAT ALL ANSWER THEIR ORACLE** (hb_bignum_length 845, hb_coexpr_parked 740, hb_coexpr_refresh 207, hb_nv 80, hb_dvec_list 20, hb_dvec_data_convert 19, hb_dvec_sort_match 19, hb_big_in_aggregate 11, hb_datblk 6, hb_eval_names 4, hb_wsb_eval_define 2, hb_blob_span_defer 1). A DEAD SPILL SLOT READS BYTE-IDENTICALLY TO A LIVE ONE. The 1954 is inside the line's own summary sentence so it can never be quoted as a defect count. The `cto` reached the same conclusion from the emitter end in the same hour: *s_raw_heap = 0 on the spine is not reachable by curing save records, because the walk range is live by ADDRESS and only the emitted code knows what is live by DATA.*
  - ⛔ **REFUTED #1 (the coo's, relayed by hq_snobol4): "off < 0 carrying a heap word takes the reporter gate to a NAMED RED".** In `gc_walk_range` the words below a frame's region go to `gc_walk_words(p, base, ...)` with `base` clamped to at least `p`, so **every** spine word in an inter-frame gap has a negative offset BY CONSTRUCTION. Arm 7 of that very gate prints its ratcheted residual as `-120:t215 x6 -120:t2 x1` on `hb_defer_subject.sno`, and on the cto's `e067b9ce9` the survivor is PROVEN DEAD by two watchpoints. **The rule reds that gate on origin today.** Also corrected: the lost word lands in `s_raw_heap`/`divergence` on the `[GC-WALK]` line, NOT in `map_only` on `[GC-MAPS]` -- a gate keyed to `map_only` would never have fired. The coo withdrew it and confirmed the grep themselves.

  - ⛔⭐⭐ **CEO-972 FIRED ON ITS FIRST AFTERNOON AND WHAT IT CAUGHT WAS A FALSE POSITIVE, BECAUSE THE ASM SHIM MANUFACTURES THE r0 THE ceo AND I HAD BOTH CERTIFIED DID NOT EXIST.** We each read the C level and concluded `rt.c:106` was the only non-null `r0` caller -- true of C callers, and INCOMPLETE. `rt_asm_helpers.S:79-92` tests `rdx` and, when the caller passed NULL, substitutes `leaq 24(%rsp)` -- a pointer to its OWN SAVED r13 -- before calling in; six pushes plus `sub 8` put the floor at `rsp+8` and the saved r13 at `rsp+24` exactly. That is correct for an **integer** -- which is what the packed spine word is, and what my landing-6 commit gives as its justification in as many words -- and **fatal for a pointer**, because the block moves, the cell nobody registered is never rewritten, and the box jumps through the stale address. I wrote the condition and did not carry it to the second road. The cto found it A/B in a clean worktree with no E involved: `hb_dvec_sort_match.sno` rc=139 at 1 MB stress 1 on `2be0f91a2`, OK on `41323bc8e` before my polls. **Reproduced here before touching anything** -- rc=139 at stress 1, 3 and 5 in mode 3, green at stress 0 -- because a blocker is measured on my own tree before it is believed.

Exactness is what lets the tag be decided instead of guessed.

Tagging it `DT_P` unconditionally and leaning on `gc_blk_of` returning null for a code address is shorter, and is **the conservative read Lon's rule forbids** -- so it is not what this does. One compare and one short forward branch: **no `cmov` and no `setcc`, because the encoder has neither mnemonic in either medium** and inventing one was not this landing's job.

- **CFO-100 (2026-09-18 21:0x CDT, cfo; LON'S IN-CHAT WORD, MODE CEO) -- `mint_op` IS REMOVED AND `src_node` IS 24 BITS; `SCRIP b9b184ffa`.**
  - ⭐⭐ **LON, in-chat to the cfo, verbatim: *"Remove mint_op since it is derivative."*** He reached it by asking two questions in order -- is `src_node` more useful than `mint_op`, and would 3 bytes hold most all programs -- and the answer to the first is **structural: `src_node` SUBSUMES `mint_op`.** Given a dense node id you can recover the op, the line and the box family (`scrip --dump-bb` prints id, kind and stmt for every box); the op is derivable from the location and the location is not derivable from the op. **The tree was storing one fact twice inside a 16-byte ABI-pinned struct.** `DESCR_t` is now `{v:1, src_node:3, slen:4, union:8}`.
  - ⛔⭐ **AND IT IS NOT A RE-WALK OF A DEAD PROPOSAL, WHICH I CHECKED BEFORE CUTTING RATHER THAN AFTER.** `descr_tags.inc` records three proposals killed under CEO-719, one of them *"src_node cannot hold the stamp because it is the BB node id and 16 bits is already too small"*. **That proposal folded the OP STAMP INTO src_node; this DELETES the op stamp and gives src_node the freed byte, so it CURES the very shortage the objection named.** The block now says so, so nobody re-litigates it from the old text.
  - ⭐ **MEASURED HEADROOM RATHER THAN ASSERTED** (`--dump-bb` box counts): **beauty.sno is the densest program in the corpus at 12812 boxes over 618 lines, 20.7 boxes/line** -- **19.6 % of the old 16-bit field and 0.08 % of the new one.** The old field saturated at **~3170 lines** of beauty-density SNOBOL4, an ordinary large program. The whole graded SNOBOL4 suite at that density would be ~402000 boxes, **2.4 % of a 24-bit space**. micro.icn 3937/1043, icon_parser 3357/461, porter 2637/437, recognizer 2496/591, tgrlink 1666/415.
  - ⛔⭐ **TWO CORRECTIONS I MADE AGAINST MYSELF, BOTH WITHIN THE HOUR, BOTH BEFORE THEY REACHED CODE.** (1) I told Lon `src_node` was **stamped nowhere**; it is stamped at `bb_lit_scalar.cpp:20`, and my grep missed it because **the template writes a SHIFTED CONSTANT, never the field name**. (2) I first read `mint_op` as free capacity from a C-side count of 3 writes -- but **a TEMPLATE stamps it**, folded into the same 32-bit `mov` immediate as the tag, so it costs **zero extra instructions**. A grep for a field name does not find a field written as an immediate.
  - ⭐ **ONE SITE, ONE IMMEDIATE, ZERO COST.** `lit_tag_imm` already OR-ed both stamps into the tag's own `mov`; it now folds only the node id, at `<<8` instead of `<<16`. **Saturation to `DESCR_SRC_NODE_OVERFLOW` is kept and widened to `0xFFFFFF`, because a WRAPPED id aliases two nodes and yields a CONFIDENTLY WRONG attribution -- strictly worse for a debugging tool than no attribution.**
  - ⛔ **WHY THE DEFAULT PATH CANNOT CHANGE, argued AND then verified rather than argued alone:** stamping is off unless `SCRIP_DESCR_STAMP=1`, and with it off bits 8-31 are zero under BOTH layouts -- **the three bytes are READ differently; nothing STORED changes.** Verified: `sizeof=16`, `v@0 src_node@1 slen@4 union@8`, roundtrip at 12812 and at 2^24-1, saturation above it, **word 0 packing as `0xABCDEF02`** for `v=0x02 src_node=0xABCDEF`. Emitted stamp decoded: **259 = 0x103** is DT_I node 1, **770 = 0x302** is DT_S node 3.

- **CFO-96 (2026-09-18 09:1x -> 10:1x CDT, cfo; THE LOOP, MODE EXECUTIVE, CEO-847/848/850) -- TWO LANDINGS, AND THE FIRST ONE CAME FROM TAKING SERIOUSLY A LINE THE ceo THREW AWAY AT THE END OF A TELEGRAM.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE ceo WROTE "that is a row for whoever wants it and I think it is bigger than it looks" ABOUT `WARN := -w`, AND IT WAS.** Makefile line 46 sets warnings off and reaches `CBASE`, `CXXRT` and all four runtime object rules, so no compiler in this tree has ever reported an implicit declaration. Their find was `prolog_driver.c` calling `lp_s_expr` with no prototype, truncating a `tree_t *` to 32 bits -- latent for years because libc's brk heap sits below 4 GiB, **fatal the moment the arena moved to mmap at 0x7ffff**. ⛔ **THE ARGUMENT THAT MADE IT URGENT IS ABOUT THEIR SWEEP, NOT ABOUT prolog_driver.c**: 1880 sites now call NEW functions, and any converted file without the declaring header gets the same bug with both the arena and the heap high. I said that to them BEFORE building it rather than presenting a finished gate.
  - ⛔ **MY FIRST CENSUS WAS NOT FAITHFUL AND I THREW IT AWAY RATHER THAN REPORTING IT.** Compiling every `.c` with the UNION of every `-I` picks up the wrong headers: 210 spurious errors, inventing findings and masking others in both directions. **The real per-file commands come from `make -n -B`**, replayed with `-w` swapped for the three truncating classes and `-c`/`-o`/`-MMD`/`-MP` stripped for `-fsyntax-only`, so it writes no object and no `.d` sidecar and cannot disturb an in-flight build. **94 TUs, 55 sites: 25 implicit declarations over 13 files, 12 int-from-pointer, 18 incompatible pointer assignments.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE FINDING OF THE SITTING, AND THE ceo CALLED IT BIGGER THAN THE EIGHTEEN ASSIGNMENTS: `core.c`'s function-block hash chain declared `struct _FuncEntry *next;` and THAT STRUCT IS MENTIONED EXACTLY ONCE IN THE WHOLE TREE, AT THAT LINE.** Never defined, anywhere. The chain's link pointed at a phantom type, so all 18 assignments across it were unchecked -- and **`_func_buckets` is the table GC-5 rung 2 is blocked on**, so a root walk traversing that field could not have been type-checked at all, and with warnings off a wrong walk would have compiled silently. **`_VarEntry` twelve lines above is the same shape and is correct and self-referential**, which is exactly what the rename lost. A collection compacts the proc-table block DOWN (`0x7ffe6d60bfb0 -> 0x7ffe6d60a230`) and rewrites that rooted slot ONCE, correctly; `g_bb_labels.data` is NOT relocated because nothing roots it; the next `lc_vec_push` writes a label entry THROUGH THE STALE POINTER into the relocated table. `rt_gc_root_args` then reads `pr->nparams`, gets a label-name rodata pointer, and walks 90136 entries off the end. ⭐ **THE TELL, and it is cheap to re-check:** entry 1's NAME changes identity between the first collection and the crash (`LBL__shf9` -> `RETURN`), and an append-only table cannot do that -- so the block is being written by someone else.   - ⭐ **THE MECHANISM, from SCRIP_ZD_DIAG=1 and the .s, not inferred:** `OUTPUT = SIZE('ab') ~DIFFER('a','a')` plans the statement's gamma chain as one run (SIZE stores to the spine, `op_zres=1`, `[rsp+0]`, never its frame slot); the negation's omega target (LIT '' -> BINOP -> ASSIGN) is an omega-head run REFUSED with why=opnd (a run may only read operands produced inside it); the refused ASSIGN is emitted unplanned and reads the FRAME slot (+64, IR_CALL result) while DIFFER's omega exit unwinds the operand literals' beta chain and pops all 80 bytes. '' or a garbage integer prints. A literal survives only through the concat-with-null fold re-materialising it inside the omega run; a builtin call, a user call, a variable, a parenthesised concat, a trailing literal and ~IDENT all lose (13 labelled variants against `sbl -bf`). Among 35 planner switches only SCRIP_ZD_ONLY=0 (plan nothing) was green. The xop rbp-frame channel (4b6110f6a) is blob-only and main's frame is rsp-addressed: not the tool. CURE, 7 lines in zd_plan after a run is built: cut the run before any 16-byte producer that is not an xop member and has a consumer outside the run whose op is not IR_MATCH_*, to a fixpoint (cascades back through literals); SCRIP_ZD_CONSUMER_CUT=0 restores the old plan.

- **CFO-83 (2026-09-16 11:28 -> 14:05 CDT, cfo; THE LOOP, Lon's "run THE LOOP") -- THE CEO-773 ROW DONE: THE aisnobol CRASH WAS NEVER ABOUT THE LIST, IT WAS A PROGRAM THAT READS &STCOUNT RUNNING EVERY STATEMENT 48 BYTES BELOW THE SPINE BASE THE UNPLANNED HALF ASSUMES. SCRIP `dca41cdf6`; four rows minted; Rebus 43/43 rewritten on it.**
  - ⭐⭐ **THE ABLATION, SEVEN GENERATIONS OF WITNESSES, EACH ONE KILLING A HYPOTHESIS:** TEST m3 died at a jump into a string constant in libscrip_rt.so and m4 died in the collector 465,460 frames deep -- both at the first list print. Not the list: `|CONS('A',NIL)` alone crashed. Not the DEXP-minted function: a plain DEFINE'd ATOM crashed too once DEXP had RUN. Not DEXP's CODE() or its DEFINE: its one statement `NAME = IDENT(NAME,'LAMBDA') "LAMBDA..." &STCOUNT "."`. Not the failure: `Z = &STCOUNT` alone, anywhere in the program, arms the crash. &STNO and &LASTNO do; &ANCHOR, &MAXLNGTH, &FNCLEVEL do not. THE READ IS A COMPILE-TIME MODE FLIP: lower_snobol4.c puts every statement behind a SNO$STMT dispatch (two literals and a CALL, 48 bytes of zeta pushes) and zd_plan let those bytes live through the statement. Planned boxes were told and popped them at the exits; the boxes the planner does not arm -- the gate of `~call`, a refused run, the half after a forward failure -- assume the plain base. So `FN (~ATOM(L) ATOM(CDR(L))) :F(FRETURN)` popped a string descriptor as FRETURN's continuation. SPITCORE mints every SNOLISP function through that DEXP statement; the entire library ran in that mode.
  - **THE CURE IS FOUR LINES IN THE PLANNER AND THE FIRST TWO CURES WERE WRONG:** (1) "pop to the statement base on an in-statement ω exit" cured the bare-statement shape and broke `:F(goto)`, RETURN and END exits -- exits must pop the residue; (2) classifying the TARGET fixed those and left `F = ~ATOM(L)` dead, because the unplanned success path never pops the residue at all, so RETURN popped garbage. The design was the defect: the residue must not exist. At STATEMENT_BEGIN the preceding CALL's γ pop grows by the depth so far and the depth restarts at zero; mark mode is then byte-for-byte the plain lowering from STATEMENT_BEGIN on. The second face fell out of the same walk: a run spanning a statement and the BLANK statement after it carried the match watermark across, and the blank END popped 64 bytes it never pushed (z1: `P IDENT(LBL) BREAK('(') . LBL`, blank line, RETURN -- crashed on origin in BOTH modes, 6 of 6 once the loop was long enough to stop the coin).   - ⭐ **NOT ONE EMITTED INSTRUCTION MOVES, IN ANY OF THE SEVEN LANGUAGES, AND THAT IS A PROOF BY CONSTRUCTION RATHER THAN A SAMPLE:** three runtime files, no template, lowerer, emitter or IR file. The only shared-node cost is **three instructions inside `.Lav_nametrap`**, the NAMETRAP arm of `rt_assign_var` in `rtx_icnvar.s`, so a plain variable assignment in any language pays nothing and only an element store pays a load and a predicted branch; `c_rt_table_assign_fast` folds the flag into its existing `g_gc_pending` test, adding a load and **no branch**. `g_sno_etrace_n` is **zero for every program that never traces an element**.
  - ⛔ **A SPELLING IS NOT AN IDENTITY, AND IT SCORED THE FAILURE BEFORE THE FIX WENT IN:** keyed on `A<2>` alone, a rebind `A = ARRAY(3)` then `A[2] = 9` **traced the new array's cell** where the oracle is silent -- a name-of binds to the CELL. The registry entry carries the container's **creation serial** (`ARBLK_t.id` / `TBBLK_t.id`, a serial and not a pointer, so the collector cannot invalidate it) and the store tap compares it. That arm is arm `rebind` in the gate, permanently.
  - ⛔ **`VARVAL_fn` ON A NAMETRAP DOES NOT RETURN THE EMPTY STRING -- IT RETURNS THE CELL'S VALUE.** The row recorded it as empty because the cell it measured was **null**. My first cut consulted `VARVAL_fn` first, so the element branch was skipped for every cell already written to and **STOPTR looked cured and was not**. A claim measured on one state of the data is a claim about that state, and the row carried it as a fact about the function.

Root cause measured: `core.h` has `NAMEVAL` (a name STRING) and `NAMEPTR` (a POINTER to the cell); a name-of-element is a NAMEPTR, so it has no string, while `trace_tab` is keyed by name — `_TRACE_` takes `VARVAL_fn`, gets `""`, refuses. ⛔ **ITS DEBUG PRINT SITS AFTER THAT GUARD**, so `SCRIP_DEBUG_TRACE=1` shows no entry line and the call looks like it never arrived. **I believed that for ten minutes before reading the source.** An "entry" trace that is not at the entry is worse than none.

Its DONE-WHEN cuts the want from the live oracle, is **RED at mint in both modes**, and **REFUSES rc=2 if m4 fails to build**, so the neighbouring-line problem can never be misread as this row's red. Two side divergences recorded rather than chased: `TRACE('A')` returns the name where SPITBOL returns null, and `.A[2]` concatenates as empty where the oracle raises ERROR 008.

SCRIP `47e8744ee`.** ⭐ **THE DEFECT WAS A DISCARDED VALUE, NOT A MISSING FEATURE, and the dumps said so before I touched anything:** the AST is CORRECT -- `(TT_SEQ (TT_SCAN (TT_VAR S) (TT_QLIT hel)) (TT_QLIT X))` -- and `sx_lower`'s `case TT_SCAN` lowered the match and then set its result to NULL, so the operator had no value in the general expression lowerer. `--dump-ir` on hq_I's three-line witness shows the consequence exactly: VAR, MATCH_BEGIN, MATCH_LIT, MATCH_END and **nothing else** -- no ASSIGN box for OUTPUT, no concatenation ever built, MATCH_END with no successor. The only reason the operator ever appeared to work is a **special case in `case TT_ASSIGN`** that rewrites a right-hand side which is ENTIRELY a TT_SCAN into a conditional-assignment capture into the assignment target, which is why `R = (S ? 'hel')` was fine and `OUTPUT = (S ? 'hel') 'X'` was not. The cure gives TT_SCAN that same capture into a compiler temp (SCV$n) and hands back a read of it, so it COMPOSES; the special case is untouched. ⭐ **THE SECOND HALF IS THE ONE NOBODY WOULD PREDICT AND IT IS THE REASON TO MEASURE AFTER EVERY EDIT RATHER THAN AT THE END:** with TT_SCAN yielding a value the gate went 8 red to 4 red -- the right-hand shapes and the function-argument shape came right -- and `OUTPUT = 'X' (S ? 'hel')` STILL printed `hel`. **EVERYTHING EVALUATED BEFORE A MATCH LOSES ITS SLOT**, measured identically on a literal, on a variable and on two literals, so it is slot-general and not a quirk of one node kind. `sx_binop` now spills its left operand into a named temp (SCL$n) when the right subtree contains a TT_SCAN, because a value that must live across a match cannot live in a slot; the spill is reached ONLY by shapes that were broken before, so no working program's evaluation order moves. ⛔ **THE CLASS WAS WIDER THAN THE WITNESS AND ONE MEMBER WAS A CRASH:** as the right operand of a concatenation it was silent; **as the LEFT operand it ABORTED WITH A CORE DUMP** through the emit_drive guard at emit.cpp:1600, the binop having been handed a null operand; as a function argument (`SIZE(S ? 'hel')`) silent; both sides at once silent; and a FAILED match correctly fails the statement, now a CONTROL arm so no later cure can quietly turn a failed match into the null string. **The silent member is what hid the crash** -- nobody writes the crashing shape while the common one prints nothing. ⛔ **ONE RUNNER ONE BOARD REFUSED ME BY NAME AND I DID NOT WORK AROUND IT.** This is the SHARED snobol4/snocone lowerer, so I wanted both masters as a regression net; the harness refused (*a master suite run is a board and seat cfo is not the coo*) and its refusal NAMED my verdict: the row DONE-WHEN plus the gates I touched plus `make preflight`. I did **not** set `S4E_ONE_RUNNER_OVERRIDE`. Instead I measured what is mine, **pre-cure against post-cure rather than against refs**: every master entry that uses the operator -- **377 snobol4 and 43 snocone -- ZERO MOVED**, including the one that segfaults, which segfaults identically either side; the ladder 350/350 unchanged over 175 witnesses; 30 of 30 wired sno behaviour gates green; preflight 36 arms 0 red. ⭐⛔ **AND A MEASUREMENT TRAP WORTH MORE THAN THE CURE, routed to the coo:** grading master entries by running `scrip` on an extracted file and diffing the extracted ref gives **261 false diffs out of 377**, because the entries need the harness's sidecars -- wantrc, stdin, argv, and the `modes` column that sends some entries to `--dump-ast` instead of a run. A naive run has none of them. That is a live argument FOR one runner one board, not against it: the board is the only instrument that grades a master correctly, and it is the coo's. The pre-cure/post-cure comparison sidesteps the whole question because it is instrument-independent -- it asks only whether MY EDIT moved anything. ⭐ **AND ONE ROW MINTED RATHER THAN LEFT IN MAIL:** hq_U handed me a diagnosis (kalah-opening-search dies error 239 at line 696, NOT the want-name class it was filed as). I re-measured rather than took it, minted `snobol4-snoflake-kalah-opening-search-returns-error-239-where-the-oracle-prints-the-board` rank 2 FREE with the program as its own DONE-WHEN proven RED at mint, and added two things the report did not have: **the stdin recipe is two lines, 4 then 3** -- with no stdin BOTH implementations stop at the first prompt and the defect is INVISIBLE -- and **line 696 is not a CODE call at all** but INITBRD's own `:(RETURN)`, so the error fires on a function return in the CALLER, a different suspect from the one the SETPATH line suggests. **Four candidate isolations are recorded in the row as already ruled out** (a bare goto into a CODE fragment; a fragment assigning through a DATA field function; that fragment looping back to its host function's entry; a two-level caller doing its own RETURN after two such calls) so the next seat does not start by re-deriving four negative results.

  - ⛔ **TWO OF FIVE DIE UNDER THE ORACLE ITSELF, SAME CLASS, ONE NEW:** fannkuch `Run-time error 122` (three-argument `insert` on a LIST is Unicon's; the ceo had measured the number, this diagnoses it) and reverse-complement `Run-time error 106` (`stat()` is Unicon's, `&null` under Icon 9.5 -- NOT in the ceo's brief, found at conversion). SCRIP dies with the SAME number at the SAME line on both, which is the right answer for an Icon compiler. Both are OUTSIDE in `OUTSIDE_ARIZONA_BASELINE.tsv` beside the sources, class `ORACLE_CONTRACT_NOT_IMPLEMENTED`, the oracle's own message quoted, and the gate RE-ASKS iconx every run and REFUSES if a row's reason stops being true. ⭐ Not done, by the ceo's fannkuch ruling ("outside until diagnosed, not a SCRIP defect"): a two-line standard-Icon read loop would bring reverse-complement inside and a four-line list-insert helper would bring fannkuch inside -- both edit the meat, so neither without a ruling; the cost of five graded is in the baton.
  - ⛔⭐ **THE ONE SCRIP DEFECT, AND IT WORE A DISGUISE:** mandelbrot at 64 differed at byte 10 with nearly every pixel bit SET where the oracle's were clear -- the exact look of an escape-test or bit-packing bug. Two probes of those constructs matched the oracle line for line; the real kernel with rows printed through `image()` matched at 64. What differed was the raw `writes`: **SCRIP's stream was SHORTER** (13 vs 15 bytes at w=8). Pinned in a five-line witness: `write`/`writes` of a string with embedded NULs print ONE byte under us and five under iconx, both modes, while `*s` and `image` are right -- the stdout/stderr path of the shared `BID_write` body went through `out_write_str` (`fputs`), while the FILE branch three lines up had been length-aware since `7fbb787eb` (iobig, 09-07). One-line cure (length-aware for any destination when the string carries a NUL within its length; a NUL-free console string takes the path it always took). **Compare LENGTHS before contents when a binary ref differs** -- `wc -c` would have cost one minute where the arithmetic probes cost twenty. - **CFO-50 (2026-09-11 16:0x CDT, cfo, CEO-545 jcon tracing) -- tracing.icn 68 -> 56 DIFFERING LINES, BOTH MODES, cxtrace HOLDING AT 0/0, SCRIP `4199135c3`.** Every call image and every outcome verb in tracing.icn is now byte-exact against the .std. ⭐ ONE DEFECT WITH TWO FACES, AND THE CONDITION IS NOT THE ONE ANYONE GUESSED: not "the call is variadic" but **"the procedure CONTAINS a `suspend`"**. Five ablation witnesses settled it and all five are now byte-identical to iconx 9.5.25a -- a variadic NON-generator images its collected list and returns correctly, and adding a SINGLE `suspend` to the SAME procedure breaks the argument image AND the outcome verb together. The cause is that a generator is entered through the N-2 region path: (1) its CALL event is raised CALLER-side in `rt_proc_call_prologue_lex` from the INCOMING argument count, so the tail list that same function had just collected into `g_call_args[nparams-1]` was never read and `trace_print_icon` padded the slot with `&null`; (2) its `return` is lowered as YIELD-THEN-RETIRE -- `bb_return` parks omega in the resume slot and jumps gamma -- so the activation's only exit tap was omega's, which reports a failure. ⛔ hq_S read face (2) as "a `&null` return is reaching IS_FAIL, so returning null is indistinguishable from failing" and that was WRONG in a way worth recording: `vproc(1)` returning **1** traced as `failed` too, so no value was reaching IS_FAIL at all and the renderer was never the boundary. Their instinct to rule out the frontend was right; their placement of the defect was one layer off. THE LESSON IS THE ABLATION, not the diagnosis: two witnesses that differ by one `suspend` named the condition in one build where reading the renderer could not.
  - **THE CURE IS THREE HUNKS AND ADDS NO MACHINERY TO THE HOT PATH:** `rt.c` reports the DECLARED parameter count (the padding loop and the vararg-tail collection immediately above had already made those slots well-defined -- the data was there and the count was wrong); `bb_return` hands the return tap a fourth argument, `rbp`, which is the region header H and is the SAME value omega reads; `emit.cpp`'s omega tap calls `rt_trace_gen_fail_hook` with that rbp, which suppresses exactly ONE fail per recorded return and is otherwise `rt_trace_fail_hook` verbatim. The record is bounded at 256 and is only ever written while tracing is on.

I kept their spelling WHOLE and dropped mine, on their argument not my preference: theirs reads the value from `FRQ(0)/FRQ(8)` rather than the pushed pair, names the procedure from `op_activate_proc`, and deliberately does NOT reuse `rt_trace_return_hook` so the generator's own gamma/omega ports keep sole ownership of `&FNCLEVEL`. I added only the fourth argument. ⛔ THEIR COMMIT NAMED MY HALF AS UNCURED IN ITS OWN MESSAGE ("written up as not-established rather than folded in") and their gate CANNOT SEE IT, because it bounds its generator with `\ 2` so the post-return resume never happens -- which is exactly how a gate can be green over the defect it sits next to. Mine drives to exhaustion, is wired BESIDE theirs rather than instead of it, and the two gate names differ by one word, so both Makefile entries now say in plain words which shape each one reaches.

It reads as a rendering nit and is not. The line came from the global `g_line`, which is an EXECUTION stamp emitted per STATEMENT rather than a property of the construct raising the event, and it had TWO independent holes. (1) It was never restored when a call RETURNED, so an event reported whatever line the callee last executed -- `recurse returned 13` printed 32, which is func's own return line, where iconx prints 24, and the second call under the `every` on line 10 printed 32 because the first call left the stamp in the callee. `rt_trace_call_hook_f` ALREADY records the caller's g_line into `g_icn_act` at entry, which is exactly why CALL events were the ones coming out right; that record is now read back at the END of the return and fail hooks, after the event is raised. (2) `parse_ctrl` captured the source line and THREW IT AWAY -- `int line = p->cur.line;` followed by `(void)line;` -- so every EXPRESSION-position return, suspend and fail carried no line at all, while `parse_stmt`'s loop gave one to the identical construct in statement position. A return in statement position had a line; the same return inside an if-then-else did not, which is why a call on line 21 reported 20. ⭐ BOTH CURES REUSE MACHINERY THAT ALREADY EXISTED and add none: the activation record the call hook already writes, and the `icn_line_hook` the TT_ACTIVATE arm already uses, under `want_lines`, which is set ONLY when the program mentions &trace -- so no non-tracing program changes. ⛔ THE 46 MISMATCHED POSITIONS FELL 46 -> 32 -> 11 ACROSS THREE MEASURED STEPS, and the middle one is the lesson: adding the lowering hooks alone moved NOTHING, because the guard is `t->line > 0` and the parser had already discarded the line. The lowerer was not why the node had no line; the PARSER was. hq_S hit the same wall from the other side and read `mov rcx, 0` in the emitted asm for it.

⭐ **hq_C's SPLIT WAS THE WHOLE CLUE:** every DELETE line `[ok]`, every INSERT line failing with elements not generated and elements generated twice. `table_icn_nth` sorted the entire set into iconx bucket order on EVERY step and memoised a POSITION in that vector — an insert that sorts ahead of the cursor shifts every later position by one (an original is skipped), and an insert that RESIZES the table changes `icn_mask`, so every element lands in a different bucket and the memoised position points into a wholly different order (elements already yielded come back). A delete never moves a position forward, which is why the delete arm stayed green and hid it for a day. ⛔ It also pinned a fresh vector per step and never freed it: my first witness, written without gener's own `limit` clause, exhausted a 512 MB heap — the reduction has to keep the limit.
⭐ **THE CURE IS A SUCCESSOR WALK, NOT A SNAPSHOT.** A step takes the smallest live element whose `(segment, slot, hash, insertion-order)` key is greater than the remembered one, under the mask captured when the walk began: an element inserted ahead of the cursor is simply never visited, one inserted behind it is, and a resize cannot reorder what is already past — iconx's own generation semantics. No allocation per step at all (the old path allocated and pinned one per step); the mask, the last key and a validity flag sit in `TBBLK_t` beside the existing `gen_idx`. ⛔ My first cut WAS a snapshot (indices into the insertion-order vector, malloc/free per generation) and it was wrong in the same way for a resize — the successor walk is the shape that needs no order to be stable.

- **CFO-39 (2026-09-10 09:4x → 11:0x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET, Icon only) — CEO-496, LON's QUESTION ("Why are we slower than an interpreter when we are a true compiler?"): CSET MEMBERSHIP IS A BIT TEST — concord IN MODE 4 FROM 0.56x OF iconx TO PARITY (SCRIP hash in the LEDGER line below, on the coo's registry index 3bbdfc8c7 as CEO-503 ordered).**
⭐ **THE MEASUREMENT FIRST.** The DONE-WHEN as minted on origin c8701b17e: concord m4 **75 ms vs iconx 42 ms (0.56x)** at load 6; the empty program iconx 2.5 ms, m4 3.8 ms. perf on the m4 binary (`/usr/lib/linux-tools-6.8.0-138/perf`; the `/usr/bin/perf` wrapper has no binary for this kernel) agreed with the ceo: 31% in `kw_cset_len`. ⭐ **THE SHAPE UNDER IT:** `many(&letters)` lowers to a `LIT_CHARSET` — the scan box's literal arm holds the characters as a read-only string the registry NEVER sees, so every character's `rt_icn_cset_member` fell through the pointer miss to the strcmp scan of every registered cset; and the loop called `rt_scan_needle` AND the member test PER CHARACTER. The runtime side of the fix is the coo's now (CEO-503: their pointer-and-content index landed first, deal 378 → 138 ms); mine goes on top: a 32-byte bit table beside every registry entry (`kw_cset_bits`, filled on append and on a length change), `rt_icn_cset_member` one bit test, and — the part that removes the registry from the hot path entirely — the LITERAL arms of `bb_scan_many` and `bb_scan_upto` seal the 256-bit table into read-only slots 3..6 at emit time (`x86_ro_seal_q`, both media) and test inline: `lea rip`, `movzx`, `bt [rdi], esi`, `jnc`. No call, no name, no registry per character — iconx's own shape.
⭐ **THE SECOND FIND WAS NOT A CSET AT ALL.** With csets fixed, `SCRIP_BID_PROF=1` named the hottest builtin by count: `ICN$LINE`, 7438 calls per concord run — the first statement of EVERY procedure body carried an `IR_CALL` to the builtin dispatcher to set `g_line` and `g_file` (the trace file column). It is now an `IR_LINE_MARK` carrying the file name; `bb_line_mark` stores both inline from a read-only string slot. `&trace` output is byte-identical (the trace witness and seven probes, both modes).
⭐ **RESULT: concord m4 30 ms vs iconx 30 ms (1.00x), outputs identical**, 0.94x–1.03x across runs on a box at load 5–7 — parity within the noise; the DONE-WHEN's `≤` PASSES on most runs and reads RED by 1 ms on some. Reported as parity, not as a pass: the ceo rules on the margin. What remains, measured: kernel 14% + loader (the 1.3 ms empty-program gap is `do_lookup_x`/`_dl_relocate_object` over a 31 MB `.so` with 2485 relocations, and page faults — not our code), `str_concat_d` 10% + `fracdigit` 4% (a GC safepoint and two bignum tests per concat), the builtin dispatcher 5.4% (the jump table covers 127 bids; `string`/`write`/`right` are in it), `kw_cset_find_ptr` 3.3% + `rt_icn_cset_register` 2.1% (the LIT_CHARSET box re-registers its literal on every evaluation — cut 2), `rt_substr` 4.4%.
⛔ **TRIED AND REVERTED, NAMED SO NOBODY TRIES IT AGAIN BLIND:** `__attribute__((always_inline))` on the `IS_*_fn` descriptor predicates in `core.h` (they are calls at `-O0`, 2–4% of the profile) BROKE MODE 3 for every program of every language — SIGSEGV in `rt_outer_call_delta0`, the slab entry — while mode 4 was fine; Gimpel read m3 0/116 on that build before I caught it with a one-line smoke. Cause not chased (the compiler binary and the `.so` see different copies of an inlined function; the slab wiring evidently takes an address). Also named: `-Wl,-Bsymbolic-functions` on the runtime `.so` for the loader cost — my trial linked the whole object dir and hit duplicate symbols; link it from `RT_PIC_OBJS` next time.

- **CFO-38 (2026-09-10 09:2x → 10:0x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET, Icon only) — THE CTO's TWO TRACE HALVES CURED AND LANDED (SCRIP hash in the LEDGER line below): THE CALL LINE PRINTS ONCE, AT THE RIGHT DEPTH, WITH EVERY PARAMETER, AND A LIST OR RECORD IS IMAGED AS iconx IMAGES IT; `&level` WAS ONE TOO HIGH ON EVERY SHORT-ARITY OR VALUE CALL.**
⭐ **THE ROOT WAS A DOUBLE COUNT, NOT A FORMAT.** `&trace := -1; f(1)` with `procedure f(a,b,c)` printed `f(1)` and then `| f(1,&null,&null)` one bar deeper, and `&level` inside read 3 where iconx says 2; `p := f; p(1,2,3)` the same even at full arity. A gdb watchpoint on `rt_k_level` in the mode-4 binary gave the pairing: the C open path (`rt_proc_call_prologue_lex`, reached by short-arity `open_detN`, by value calls through `rt_proc_call_gen_h`, and by `rt_proc_call_c_lex`) counts up and prints with the PASSED arguments; the Icon callee's own activation entry `FN__f` counts up and prints again with every PARAMETER; `f_γ`/`f_ω` count down; the C epilogue counts down again. Pascal's `FN__f` never touches the counter (its C pair is its only pair) and neither does Prolog's; a full-arity Icon call takes the direct `f_dcα` entry with no C pair at all, which is why only the C-path shapes doubled. ⛔ SNOBOL4's `&FNCLEVEL` was right throughout (1 1 0 against SPITBOL through `F(1)` and `APPLY`), so the dyn-scope pair was left exactly as it was.
⭐ **THE CURE: THE C OPEN MARKS EACH LEVEL AS CALLEE-OWNED OR NOT.** A procedure with a direct-call entry (`g_rt_dc_fns_store[idx]`, the `dcfn` the staged path jumps to with no prologue) and not a generator owns its level; for those the C open neither counts nor prints, and every C epilogue (`γ`, `ω`, `named`) consults the mark for the level it closes instead of counting down blindly — a 64K byte array indexed by level, cleared on close, sound under LIFO nesting; beyond the array the old count-and-print stands. ⛔ My first cut printed BEFORE the count and the generator line lost a bar (generators take the coexpr branch and are counted there; excluded from ownership) — the order is count then print, as it always was. **THE IMAGE HALF** is a trace-only imager in `core.c`: a top-level list prints `list_N = [e1,e2,e3,...,e(n-2),e(n-1),en]` past six elements (`[]` when empty), a top-level record `record T_N(f1,f2)`, nested aggregates in image()'s count form UNLESS empty (`list_4 = []`, `record e_1()` — iconx's own rule, probed on nine shapes), tables and sets always the count form; the return and suspend values the same. The `suspended`/`resumed` lines stay the cto's rtx-spine half (CEO-468) and are not touched.

⭐ **CEO-483 (movecorr shape B) WAS MINE FROM YESTERDAY.** The row's DONE-WHEN passed on origin 483717a0c in both modes before I touched anything: the cure is `183aee4a1` (CFO-34). The ceo's RED came from /home/claude_ceo/SCRIP at 8c8f88b1c with a 19:09 binary, six minutes before the cure landed — a stale checkout read as origin; the ceo retracted it (CEO-484). Row closed with that receipt, no diff.
⭐ **THE NEXT RED WAS ONE LINE OF ilib, AND IT WAS NOT A CALL SHAPE.** ilib through `test_icon_arizona_suite.sh` diffed on `starseq` alone (`suspend "" | (starseq(s) || !s)`): `aaab` where iconx says `aab`. Five reductions said the recursion was noise; the smallest shape is `y := m2("a")` where m2 RETURNS `s || "a"`, then `every write(y || !"ab")` prints `aaa aaab` and `write(y)` prints `aaab` (iconx: `aa`). `*y` still read 2 — the descriptor kept its length while the bytes under it grew. A gdb hardware watchpoint on `g_sxt_fr.owner` in the mode-4 binary gave the sequence, not a theory: the concat result is noted as the extension OWNER; the local store is a bare slot move that never breaks ownership; the next concat sees its left operand IS the owner and grows it in place (`rt_sxt_extend`), so every strlen reader (Icon `write` → `VARVAL_fn`) sees the grown string; and on resumption `rt_sxt_match` matched by POINTER ALONE and handed back the owner's grown length for an operand whose own length was 2 — `"aaa" || "b"`. ⛔ The inline twin `y := "a" || "a"` only LOOKED correct because two literals fold to one literal — it never tested the store. With `z := "a"; y := z || z` the local, a PARAMETER, a GLOBAL and a STATIC all fail the same way; a LIST element does not (aggregates.c breaks ownership on store). SNOBOL4 is sound: every SNOBOL4 variable is in the GVA the alias census scans, and its store path breaks ownership — `X = Z 'b'; OUTPUT = X 'c'; OUTPUT = X` and the function-param/local twin match SPITBOL both modes before and after.
⭐ **THE CURE IS A PROPERTY SWITCH, NOT A LANGUAGE BRANCH.** `rt_icn_zframe_args_install` — the Icon frame installer every procedure entry runs — calls `rt_sxt_frames_present()`, which sets the table's existing off switch (the `SCRIP_SXT_OFF` slot both the asm fast path and the C path already honour): a program with frame-resident variables cannot be alias-scanned, so it gets no in-place append. And `str_concat_d` refuses the owner match when the operand's own length differs from the noted length — a descriptor sharing the pointer with another length is a different string (a latent SNOBOL4 hazard too; kept). Cost of the switch: Icon's `s ||:= c` loop was O(n) only when nothing else held s, which is never the shape in real programs; iconx itself copies on every concat, so the boards' 8 s / 30 s ceilings did not move — IPL HANG stayed 0 in both modes.

⭐ **THE PICK AND THE MEASUREMENT.** `gener` (95 lines) is hq_C's set-generation row, so the lane's next red was `coexpr` (CRASH) — and the crash needed no co-expression at all: `&trace := -1; dummy(1)` died in `vsnprintf`'s `movaps` for any GENERATOR callee with ≥1 argument, while a non-generator callee, a zero-argument generator and the untraced call were fine (cx4/cx7/cx10, iconx refs cut). gdb, not theory: si_addr 0 (a general-protection fault, not a stack overflow), the prologue call's canonical frame address ≡ 8 mod 16 and every frame under it inheriting that, and the blob before the call reading `sub rsp,8; sub rsp,8; lea L7; push` — THREE words in the generator regime (`bcps_spine_gen_arm`: the N-2 ABI word hq_I root-caused for the CALLEE BODY's parity, the PL-CALL-ALIGN pad, and L7) pushed BEFORE `call rt_proc_call_open_det`, so that call and everything it reached (`rt_proc_call_prologue_lex → rt_trace_event_args → image → snprintf`) ran misaligned, latent until printf's aligned SSE store. **The cure is a reorder, not a new word:** the three pushes now follow the prologue call; the callee's entry layout is byte-identical (only the call moved above the words; L7 goes through rcx because rax carries the callee pointer there); both mediums, one template. ⛔ My first edit changed nothing because the L7 lea is `x86_lea_id`, not `x86("lea")` — the assertion in my own script said so and I read the line in full before the second cut.

⭐ **ICON-445: the SNOBOL4 claim `snobol4-value-call-poisons-next-lazy-comparator-oracle-quirk` is PARKED; my lane is Arizona A-L.** The FAIL list after `args`: **cfuncs coexpr errors evalx extlvals fncs gener ilib** (M-Z is hq_B's). `cfuncs` and `extlvals` both `link cfunc` and die at `loadfunc` (ERROR 022 undefined function) — a C-function loader, not a semantic; parked behind the semantics. `coexpr` segfaults at its co-expression `dummy(...)` traceback section and misorders `image` lines. `ilib` is ONE program that is a ladder: four rungs, each pinned to a ≤20-line witness against iconx BEFORE any cure, in the order the program hits them:
   (1) **`DateLineToSec` (year read as &null → today's date):** THE PORT THAT FIRED WRONG IS FOUND — `x <- 2 | 3` yields **3**, `x <- 2 | 3 | 4` yields **3** (resumed exactly once, not exhausted), `x <- 1 to 3` yields 1, and `x <- g()` with a suspending `g` SEGFAULTS. `--dump-bb` shows why: in `lower_icon.c` TT_REVASSIGN (VAR-lhs branch) the lvalue's γ is wired with `γ_to(lr, re)`, and `γ_to` routes to the target's **β** whenever the target is a generator kind — so the alternation is ENTERED through its resume port, skips its first alternative, and a procedure-call RHS resumes a frame that was never created. `:=` (TT_ASSIGN) never does this. The cure is one edge (`lc_γ_to`, the α entry, as the IDX branch already uses); the crash and the wrong value are the same wire. Witness `w_rev.icn`: 18 shapes, 11 diverge.
   (2) **`saytime` returns `0` instead of "just gone  three o'clock"** — a case arm whose body is the empty compound `{}` (`0: {}`) makes the PROCEDURE return the case value. `{}` parses as a zero-child TT_SEQ_EXPR, lowered to an IR_SUCCEED with the NULL γ the case lowering passes, and the arm wiring `else if (!bv) γ_to(be, asn)` never fires because `bv` is the SUCCEED itself — the arm's exit is never wired to the result assignment.
   (3) **`lclose` returns the list unchanged (IPL `equiv` succeeds on unequal lists)** — a `fail` inside `every … do if … then fail` inside a case arm does not fail the procedure. `--dump-ir` shows the arm's whole `every` body UNREACHED: the same case-arm wiring rewires the arm's ENTRY node's γ (`γ_to(be, asn)`, `be` = the `1` of `1 to 2`) straight to the result assignment, clobbering the body. (2) and (3) are one defect: the case lowering should lower each arm ONTO its result assignment (γ = asn, the TT_ASSIGN idiom) instead of rewiring the entry afterwards. The same rewrite idiom sits in the `break` lowering (`__break_result`) — named, not touched.
   (4) **`lfliph` overflows the stack** — `lfliph := lreverse; return lfliph(L)`: a call through a name that is ALSO a declared procedure binds to the declaration, not to the global variable the program just reassigned. An engine class (direct-call binding vs the global's value), the deepest of the four; last.

**&DUMP COMPLETENESS IS CURED, GATED, DETECTOR-PROVED AND LANDED (SCRIP `01eb996ca`; the ceo's 22:2x pivot; hq_S measured it on spitbol_testpgms test1).** Measured against `sbl -bf` (the 22:34 swapped binary, live, per CEO-440 clause f): SCRIP's dump omitted every ARRAY, TABLE, DATA, PATTERN, EXPRESSION, NAME and CODE value, printed null-valued variables and internal `EXPR$n` temps, printed OUTPUT nowhere (it lives under `_OUTPUT`), did not wrap at 120 bytes, and had no `&DUMP = 2` contents section at all; test1 wanted 13 lines SCRIP could not print. THE CURE: one creation serial shared by arrays, tables and data objects (the `#n`), stamped at every constructor — there were FOUR data-constructor sites, not two, the fourth in `src/driver/driver_data.c`, found only because the probe kept reading `#0` — with nested rows of an N-dimensional prototype handing their serial back; the ARRAY prototype text kept as given; `*X` names its temp `EXPR$n$X` so the dump prints `*X`; a by-name read of ARB/BAL/... marks it touched so the dump prints it only when the program named it (test1's `BAL = PATTERN`); and the rewritten walker with the 120-byte wrap that embedded newlines do NOT reset (SPITBOL counts bytes of the value, measured on &ALPHABET). ⭐ THE ONE I DID NOT EXPECT TO BE ABLE TO MATCH: a table's contents come out in SPITBOL's BUCKET order, and the hash turned out to be recoverable from the outside — a 1000-bucket table with an integer in every bucket makes each string key print right after its bucket's integer, and twelve probes fit exactly one model: **hash = XOR of the key's little-endian 8-byte words XOR its length, integers by magnitude, reals by bit pattern, mod the declared size, insertion order within a bucket.** ARBLK_t grew 48 → 56 bytes and the rtx layout pin moved with it (the .s reads only lo/hi/ndim/data by offset). MEASURED: five witnesses byte-identical to the oracle in both modes (cmp, not diff — the wrap and the trailing blank lines are the point); test1's dump section now differs from SPITBOL's ONLY in DIAGNOSTICS, &ERRLIMIT, &STCOUNT and &TRACE, the four counters of hq_S's traps row; SNOBOL4 master 1893/1917 FAIL=0 both modes (3 m 17 s @ load 1); Icon master 707/707; Arizona 73/90; preflight green. ⭐ THE INSTRUMENT, because nothing graded ever set &DUMP: `test_gate_sno_dump_matches_spitbol.sh`, wired into `make test` at 1.7 s, five witnesses x two modes cut live from the oracle — **10/10 green with the cure, 10/10 RED with it reverted**, proved by stash/rebuild/gate/pop/rebuild, not assumed. ⛔ TWO RACES OF MY OWN THIS SITTING, both the same shape: a source edit (even a reflow with no build) makes the binary older than the tree, and every guarded runner refuses rc=2 — the Icon boards refused once behind a rebuild and once behind a reflow. The rule I now keep: no edit under `src/` while a board is in flight, not even cosmetic. RESIDUAL, named: `.A<1>` (a NAME into an array cell) prints `NAME` because a cell reference carries no array name; and test1 itself will not flip until the traps row lands, since its four counters are that row's.
- **CFO-25 (2026-09-08 20:5x-22:4x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET) — MY OWN `seq` CURE HAD NO REGRESSION PROTECTION AT ALL; A WITNESS MINTED, DETECTOR-PROVED AND LANDED (corpus `3b10e1590`); A SECOND DEFECT FOUND WHILE MINTING IT; AND A 27-MINUTE `make test` COST ME A ONE-WORD CURE TO A RACE.**
