Had I trusted it I would have shipped a witness that proves nothing, which is the exact false-green our own gates carry a detector-proof arm to prevent. **THE GAP IS NAMED, NOT LEFT QUIET:** the family is declared in `config/MODES.tsv` as `m3,m4` (measured in both modes, not guessed from the family name, as that file's own warning requires) so it is ONE COMMAND from absorption — but I did not run that command, because `util_build_master_suite.py --additive` RENUMBERS THE WHOLE MASTER (463 insertions / 443 deletions on ALL.csv) and absorbs the other 20 undeclared families with it, including other seats' pending witnesses and `icon_display_builtin_unimplemented`. That is a denominator change belonging to whoever owns the master, not a side effect of adding one witness. ⛔ ALSO LEARNED THE HARD WAY: `--additive` WRITES WITHOUT `--write` — there is no dry run on that path, and I reverted the master twice before I understood that.
⭐ **A SECOND DEFECT, FOUND WHILE MINTING THE FIRST AND DELIBERATELY NOT PINNED: THE LIMITATION OPERATOR OVER A CONJUNCTION YIELDS EXACTLY ONE RESULT.** `((1|2|3) & 7) \ 5` gives SCRIP one 7 against Arizona's three; `((1 to 3) & 7) \ 5` the same; `(seq(3,2) & 7) \ 4` one against four. ⭐ THE CONTROL ARM IS THE POINT: `(1|2|3) \ 5` gives `1 2 3` in BOTH, so `\` alone is correct and it is losing resumption specifically over a CONJUNCTION — one defect in the limitation lowering, not a per-generator gap. Arm 4b only flags names SCRIP DISPATCHES, which is why 90 is green. A reflective list must never name a builtin the engine cannot call; if `display` is implemented it lands WITH its list entry in the same commit.

- **CFO-24 (2026-09-08 20:0x-20:5x CDT, cfo, MODE NONET) — THE RUNTIME DEFINE CLASS IS FOUR FAILURES NOT TWO, MODE 3 NEVER RUNS THE DEFINE BOX, AND THE OBVIOUS CURE REGRESSES THREE MASTER ENTRIES — ABLATED AND BACKED OUT, TREE LEFT CLEAN.** The ceo routed hq_P's class to me (two defects, one mechanism, grade on BOTH witnesses). Both hq_P witnesses reproduce verbatim. ⭐ THERE IS A THIRD SYMPTOM NOBODY NAMED: wA in MODE 4 prints `orig(x)`/`orig(x)` — the runtime redefinition performed inside a function is **silently ignored**, no crash, no diagnostic. So the class is FOUR cells (wB m3 new/new, wB m4 new/new, wA m3 orig+SIGSEGV, wA m4 orig/orig), and a seat grading only m3 fixes the crash and leaves m4 wrong with nothing saying so. THE MECHANISM, top to bottom: the crash is a jump to address 0 because `rt_sno_runtime_define` (rt.c:609) sets `p->fn = 0` and zeroes the `alpha$<name>` cell and never installs the new entry — its caller `_DEFINE_` has the entry label in hand and does not pass it; the static half bakes `body_cell$F: .quad LBL__F2` and BOTH define sites emit `lea rax,[rip+LBL__F2]`, so the first statement seals the second's body; and the IR ALREADY KNOWS BETTER — each bind node carries its own `LIT_NAME` entry and there is even an accessor, `ir_define_bind_entry()` at IR.h:197 — the dedup is in `scrip.c`'s dentry construction, which resolves the entry by looking up the FUNCTION NAME in `s2->proc_table`, one row per name. Also `bb_define.cpp:424` is a two-medium disagreement on one line: text uses the statement's label, binary uses `rt_define_query`'s per-name answer. ⭐⭐ THE FACT THAT DECIDES THE CURE, and I proved it rather than inferred it: I wrote a candidate runtime binder with a diagnostic gated on `SCRIP_DEFINE_BIND_DIAG=1`, called from the DEFINE site in role 6 which `bb_define()` dispatches for BOTH mediums. In m4 it fires twice with the right per-statement entries AND CURES wB. **In m3 it never fires at all** — the emitted call is in the asm (grep counts 2) and mode 3 never runs it. Mode 3 binds DEFINE entirely at slab-seal time, so ANY cure written only in the template moves m4 and leaves m3 byte-for-byte unchanged. ⛔ AND THE OBVIOUS CURE IS WRONG, MEASURED NOT GUESSED: making dentry use the bind node's own entry cures wB m4 and XPASSes `user_function_replace_7`, but REGRESSES `user_function_26`, `user_function_replace_10` and `user_function_replace_branch_1` in m4 — caught by the SNOBOL4 master as a control arm, against hq_U's run 29 s earlier on clean `e4ff7e9c7` which had zero unmarked m4 non-PASS. Cause: a DEFINE with NO explicit second argument must bind to the `<fname>_α` ACTIVATION, and resolving its own body label routes around it. The two arms are COUPLED — restricting the patch to explicit retargets removes the regression AND removes the cure, because wB's first DEFINE has an implicit entry. NOT CURED; candidate BACKED OUT and the tree left clean at `397b3bd22` so no seat measures a board against a half-cure. Full write-up, four-cell table, backtrace and the ablation: `FINDING-2026-09-08-cfo-the-runtime-define-class-is-four-failures-mode-3-never-runs-the-define-box-and-the-obvious-cure-regresses-three-master-entries.md`. ⭐ THE CEO'S 20:4x QUESTION IS ANSWERED AND HIS HYPOTHESIS WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the three m4 reds on my dirty tree were NOT the uncommitted Icon lowerer edit — that had already landed as `196d19e79` before the board ran — they were this DEFINE candidate in `src/driver/scrip.c`. On the clean tree all three run PASS in m4, verified by name. The control arm did its job either way. ALSO CLEARED THIS SITTING (cto's telegram, my lane): `test_gate_score_tables_agree.sh` was RED on `icon: display vendor says 71/90, grid V says 62/90` and blocking `make test` for every seat; I did not copy the number across by hand — I re-ran `test_icon_arizona_suite.sh` on the clean tree and let the runner do its own dual write, both halves now 72/90 with matching provenance, gate rc=0.

- **CFO-23 (2026-09-08 19:5x-20:2x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET -> NONET) — A REAL ICON SEMANTIC CURED THAT MOVES NO BOARD, THE PROGRAM IT WAS CHASING RE-DIAGNOSED, AND BOTH ICON ROWS RELEASED ON THE SNOBOL4 TELEGRAM (SCRIP `196d19e79`).** THE CURE: `seq()` is one of Icon's FIVE function generators (bal, find, key, seq, upto) and `icn_call_allow_gen()` in `src/lower/lower_icon.c:68` listed only three, so `is_resumable()` said no and the resume GOTO of an enclosing `suspend` was wired to the FAIL node instead of to the TO box. `suspend seq(3,2) & 7` yielded ONE 7 against Arizona's unbounded stream; `suspend seq(3,2)` alone was always right, which is why it hid. The IR is the whole proof in two lines — for `(1 to 9) & 7` the resume goto reads `8@ 2 2 GOTO` (node 2 is the TO box), for `seq(1) & 7` it read `8@ 7@ 7@ GOTO` (node 7@ is FAIL) — and `seq` and `to` lower to the SAME `IR_TO` box, so only the predicate ever differed. The IPL's `prime()` is built from `suspend 2 | ((i := seq(3,2)) & (not(...)) & i)`, so `pfactors`/`isprime`/`prdecomp`/`prime` all stopped after their second value. ⛔ I MEASURED BOTH DIRECTIONS WITH THE TWO BINARIES KEPT SIDE BY SIDE rather than trusting the after-number: Arizona 72/90, Jcon 56/82, Icon master 704/704 — IDENTICAL before and after. A correct oracle-checked cure with NO FLIP, and I am recording it as exactly that. `make test` green, 75 arms, exit 0. ⛔ `bal` DELIBERATELY LEFT OUT: its own generation is short (`every bal('+',,,"a+b+c")` gives 2 where Arizona gives `2 4`), and marking a short generator resumable turns a wrong FINITE answer into an INFINITE resumption — `bal(...) & 7` then yields 7 forever, a HANG where there was a FAIL. I added it, measured that, and backed it out. Cure bal's generation first, list it second. ⭐ I AM CORRECTING MY OWN 09-07 CENSUS LINE: `ilib` is NOT "a crash, and the crash is the finding" in the Icon library — it dies in the SHARED CALL-VALUE SPINE. `rt_call_value_resume_h` (`src/runtime/by_name_dispatch.c:1069`) dereferences `*hslot` as an `ICN_OPGEN_t *` and reads `g->magic` after testing only NON-NULL; when an `IR_CALL_VALUE` beta is entered in an activation whose ALPHA never ran, the cell holds the previous activation's handle instead of the 0 that alpha writes. Eight-line oracle-clean witness (Arizona prints A / 463410420 / B, rc=0), gdb backtrace and a full ablation — neither half alone crashes; the trigger is apply of a procedure value held in a PARAMETER, callee leaving live choice points, resumed THROUGH A LIMITATION — are in `FINDING-2026-09-08-cfo-a-call-value-resume-site-reads-a-handle-cell-its-own-alpha-never-wrote-and-segfaults-on-the-garbage.md`. ⛔ THE CRASH IS PRE-EXISTING AND I PROVED IT RATHER THAN ASSERTING IT: the baseline binary crashes on the same witness, so the `seq` cure did not cause it. But it DOES change which garbage sits in the cell — `ilib` HANGS at 125 lines without the cure and CRASHES at line 9 with it — which means **ilib's CRASH-vs-HANG verdict is not a measurement of anything until the spine is cured**, and no seat should report movement either way. ⭐ AND THE ROW'S OWN DONE-WHEN LIES: it copies only `<p>.icn`/`<p>.dat` into its scratch dir, but `io.icn` shells `ls io.[ids][tca][dnt]` and `ls io.i?n io.d?t io.s?d`, so `io` reads RED (`< io.std`) on a program that is CURED — measured in a directory containing `io.std`, io matches with ZERO differing lines. It is ceo-minted so I did not edit it; it needs one `cp "$SUITE/$p.std" .`. TRUE red set is SIX, not seven, and every one is blocked or spine-deep: fncs gener recent sorting recogn ilib. That is a real Icon semantic touching write and writes together, worth 12 of the 32 lines, and it is a change rather than a patch. (B) `sort()` orders FILE values by descriptor where Arizona orders them by image -- the same sort()-type-order item the ceo's coexpr note names. THE HONEST SHAPE OF THIS ROW NOW: of its eight remaining reds, FOUR are behind other rows (recogn/gener/recent/sorting behind 676; fncs behind the serial row), io needs (A), and ilib segfaults. The cheap ones are gone.

- **CFO-21 (2026-09-08 19:2x-19:4x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET) — io GOES 73 -> 7 DIFFERING LINES ON TWO CURES (SCRIP `809f18f9f`, `e2bb6141e`).** `rename()` had an arity and no body; then the real one: **a file argument inside `write()`/`writes()` must redirect the arguments after it**, and SCRIP only ever honoured a file in position 0, so every later file argument was written as a VALUE to the destination chosen at the start -- io.icn's four such calls came out interleaved on stdout with the files empty. ⛔ I GOT THE NEWLINE RULE WRONG ONCE AND THE MEASUREMENT CORRECTED ME: my first version emitted the newline only when the destination being left had been WRITTEN to, which is the intuitive rule. Arizona emits one on EVERY file argument except one in position 0. The case that settles it is a REPEATED file argument -- `write(">stdout", t1, ">1c", t2, t2, ">2c", &output)` puts an EMPTY LINE in t2 for the second t2, which a was-it-written rule cannot produce -- and `write(f,"S",g,"T")` proves the position-0 exemption. SCRIP now reproduces the four-call witness byte-for-byte. ⛔ AND A WRONG COUNT OF MY OWN, caught: io runs `ls io.s?d` through `system()`, so measuring it in a scratch dir WITHOUT io.std produced two diff lines that were my harness's fault, not SCRIP's. Re-measured in a faithful directory. io's remaining 7 are two further isolated defects, neither touched: sort() orders files by descriptor where Arizona orders by image, and read() does not treat `\r` as a line terminator where Arizona does. Icon master 704/704, Arizona 70/90, Jcon 55/82 -- all unchanged, io still red until its last 7 go.
- **CFO-22 (2026-09-08 19:2x-20:0x CDT, cfo, MODE QUARTET) — THE ARIZONA io TEST IS CURED, 73 DIFFERING LINES TO ZERO IN BOTH MODES (SCRIP `809f18f9f`, `e2bb6141e`, `384033ade`). Arizona 70/90 -> **71/90 BOTH MODES**, row written by the runner at `384033ade`.** Five defects, each isolated against Arizona icont BEFORE it was written: `rename()` had an arity and no body · a file argument inside `write()`/`writes()` only ever worked in position 0 · the u/t open mode was PARSED AND THROWN AWAY · `sort()` left files in insertion order · stdout was not flushed before `pclose`. ⭐ THE ONE I WANT REMEMBERED: **the u/t mode was computed and discarded.** `icn_open_cmode` already worked out `un` for u/t and used it only to append `'b'` to the C mode -- a no-op on Linux -- and dropped it ENTIRELY for a pipe, which is the case the test exercises. So read() had ONE behaviour regardless of what the program asked for. A flag that is parsed, not used, and not refused is worse than an unimplemented one: the program says `"up"`, nothing complains, and the answer is silently the other mode's. ⛔ AND THE ONLY m3/m4 DIVERGENCE IN THE TEST, named because that is the shape the one-engine rule exists to prevent: mode 4 alone printed `--- closing output pipe` AFTER the pipe's own output, because our stdout was still buffered when `pclose` let the child through. m3 passed and m4 failed on the SAME SOURCE. Cured by flushing stdout before pclose, as iconx does. ⛔ TWO MISTAKES OF MINE ON THE WAY, both caught by building: I got the write() newline rule wrong first (was-it-written instead of positional -- a REPEATED file argument settles it), and I added a house-style separator line WITHOUT its closing `*/`, which commented out `icn_open_cmode` and produced an undefined-reference at link. The separators are 200 chars wide and I had only ever seen them truncated at 150. Icon master 704/704 both modes and Jcon 55/82 both modes, unchanged throughout.

## THE CFO LOOP
1. `check` the inbox; route economy asks to Lon in chat, grants into this file the same turn.
2. The ledger line (above), every sitting and on every Lon "next".
3. The runaway census; kill only under a recorded grant.
4. `next` — the EASIEST FREE rank-0 row; cure; measure through the suite's own runner; flip to `coo`; repeat.
5. A landing = pushed + the runner's row rewritten + the ledger line; the banner verdict is computed, never typed.
